^1-612

What is that material form which is the body sense base?
The body, that is to say the sensitive matter, derived from the four great primaries, included in one's own being, invisible and with impingement; against which body, invisible and with impingement, the tangible, invisible and with impingement, has impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge; this is the body, this is the body sense base, this is the body element, this is the body faculty, this is the world, this is the door, this is the ocean, this is the white, this is the field, this is the ground, this is the hither shore, this is the empty village – this is that material form which is the body sense base.
^1-613

What is that material form which is the body sense base?
The body, that is to say the sensitive matter, derived from the four great primaries, included in one's own being, invisible and with impingement; which body, invisible and with impingement, has impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge on the tangible, invisible and with impingement; this is the body, this is the body sense base, this is the body element, this is the body faculty, this is the world, this is the door, this is the ocean, this is the white, this is the field, this is the ground, this is the hither shore, this is the empty village – this is that material form which is the body sense base.
^1-614

What is that material form which is the body sense base?
The body, that is to say the sensitive matter, derived from the four great primaries, included in one's own being, invisible and with impingement; depending on which body, in consequence of the tangible, tactile contact has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise…pe… depending on which body, in consequence of the tangible, feeling born of tactile contact…pe… perception…pe… volition…pe… tactile consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise…pe… depending on which body, having the tangible as its object, tactile contact has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise…pe… depending on which body, having the tangible as its object, feeling born of tactile contact…pe… perception…pe… volition…pe… tactile consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise; this is the body, this is the body sense base, this is the body element, this is the body faculty, this is the world, this is the door, this is the ocean, this is the white, this is the field, this is the ground, this is the hither shore, this is the empty village – this is that material form which is the body sense base.
^1-615

What is that material form which is the visible form sense base?
That material form which, derived from the four great primaries, is color and appearance, visible and with impingement—blue, yellow, red, white, black, crimson, green, mango-bud colored; long, short, small, large, round, circular, square, hexagonal, octagonal, sixteen-sided; low, high, shadow, sunlight, light, darkness, cloud, mist, smoke, dust; the color and appearance of the moon's disk, the sun's disk, stars; the color and appearance of a mirror, a gem, a conch, a pearl, lapis lazuli; the color and appearance of gold or silver; or whatever other material form there is which, derived from the four great primaries, is color and appearance, visible and with impingement; which visible form, visible and with impingement, one has seen, sees, will see, or may see with the eye, invisible and with impingement; this is visible form, this is the visible form sense base, this is the visible form element – this is that material form which is the visible form sense base.
^1-616

What is that material form which is the visible form sense base?
That material form which, derived from the four great primaries, is color and appearance, visible and with impingement—blue, yellow, red, white, black, crimson, green, mango-bud colored; long, short, small, large, round, circular, square, hexagonal, octagonal, sixteen-sided; low, high, shadow, sunlight, light, darkness, cloud, mist, smoke, dust; the color and appearance of the moon's disk, the sun's disk, stars; the color and appearance of a mirror, a gem, a conch, a pearl, lapis lazuli; the color and appearance of gold or silver; or whatever other material form there is which, derived from the four great primaries, is color and appearance, visible and with impingement; against which visible form, visible and with impingement, the eye, invisible and with impingement, has impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge; this is visible form, this is the visible form sense base, this is the visible form element – this is that material form which is the visible form sense base.
^1-617

What is that matter which is the visible object base?
That matter, derived from the four great essential elements, visible as color and luster, impinging: blue, yellow, red, white, black, crimson, green, mango-bud color; long, short, minute, coarse, round, circular, square, hexagonal, octagonal, sixteen-sided; depression, elevation, shadow, sunlight, light, darkness, cloud, mist, smoke, dust; the luster of the moon's orb, the sun's orb, star forms, a mirror's orb, gems, conches, pearls, lapis lazuli, gold, and silver. Or whatever other matter exists, derived from the four great essential elements, visible as color and luster, impinging. That matter, visible and impinging, which impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge on the eye, which is invisible and impinging. This matter is the visible object base, this is the visible object element – this is that matter, the visible object base.
^1-618

What is that matter which is the visible object base?
That matter, derived from the four great essential elements, visible as color and luster, impinging: blue, yellow, red, white, black, crimson, green, mango-bud color; long, short, minute, coarse, round, circular, square, hexagonal, octagonal, sixteen-sided; depression, elevation, shadow, sunlight, light, darkness, cloud, mist, smoke, dust; the luster of the moon's orb, the sun's orb, star forms, a mirror's orb, gems, conches, pearls, lapis lazuli, gold, and silver. Or whatever other matter exists, derived from the four great essential elements, visible as color and luster, impinging. That matter, having taken it as an object and relying on the eye, eye-contact arose, arises, will arise, or may arise; feeling born of eye-contact, perception, volition, eye-consciousness arose, arises, will arise, or may arise. That matter as its object, relying on the eye, eye-contact arose, arises, will arise, or may arise; feeling born of eye-contact, perception, volition, eye-consciousness arose, arises, will arise, or may arise. This matter is the visible object base, this is the visible object element – this is that matter, the visible object base.
^1-619

What is that matter which is the sound base?
That sound, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging: drum sound, tabor sound, conch sound, instrumental music, singing, instrumental music, clapping, manual sounds, sounds of living beings, sounds of colliding elements, wind sound, water sound, human sound, non-human sound. Or whatever other sound exists, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging. That sound, invisible and impinging, which one heard, hears, will hear, or may hear with the ear, which is invisible and impinging. This sound is the sound base, this is the sound element – this is that matter, the sound base.
^1-620

What is that matter which is the sound base?
That sound, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging: drum sound, tabor sound, conch sound, instrumental music, singing, instrumental music, clapping, manual sounds, sounds of living beings, sounds of colliding elements, wind sound, water sound, human sound, non-human sound. Or whatever other sound exists, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging. That sound, invisible and impinging, on which the ear, invisible and impinging, impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge. This sound is the sound base, this is the sound element – this is that matter, the sound base.
^1-621

What is that matter which is the sound base?
That sound, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging: drum sound, tabor sound, conch sound, instrumental music, singing, instrumental music, clapping, manual sounds, sounds of living beings, sounds of colliding elements, wind sound, water sound, human sound, non-human sound. Or whatever other sound exists, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging. That sound, invisible and impinging, which impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge on the ear, which is invisible and impinging. This sound is the sound base, this is the sound element – this is that matter, the sound base.
^1-622

What is that matter which is the sound base?
That sound, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging: drum sound, tabor sound, conch sound, instrumental music, singing, instrumental music, clapping, manual sounds, sounds of living beings, sounds of colliding elements, wind sound, water sound, human sound, non-human sound. Or whatever other sound exists, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging. That sound, having taken it as an object and relying on the ear, ear-contact arose, arises, will arise, or may arise; feeling born of ear-contact, perception, volition, ear-consciousness arose, arises, will arise, or may arise. That sound as its object, relying on the ear, ear-contact arose, arises, will arise, or may arise; feeling born of ear-contact, perception, volition, ear-consciousness arose, arises, will arise, or may arise. This sound is the sound base, this is the sound element – this is that matter, the sound base.
^1-623

What is that matter which is the odor base?
That odor, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging: root odor, wood odor, bark odor, leaf odor, flower odor, fruit odor, raw odor, putrid odor, pleasant odor, unpleasant odor. Or whatever other odor exists, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging. That odor, invisible and impinging, which one smelled, smells, will smell, or may smell with the nose, which is invisible and impinging. This odor is the odor base, this is the odor element – this is that matter, the odor base.
^1-624

What is that matter which is the odor base?
That odor, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging: root odor, wood odor, bark odor, leaf odor, flower odor, fruit odor, raw odor, putrid odor, pleasant odor, unpleasant odor. Or whatever other odor exists, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging. That odor, invisible and impinging, on which the nose, invisible and impinging, impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge. This odor is the odor base, this is the odor element – this is that matter, the odor base.
^1-625

What is that matter which is the odor base?
That odor, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging: root odor, wood odor, bark odor, leaf odor, flower odor, fruit odor, raw odor, putrid odor, pleasant odor, unpleasant odor. Or whatever other odor exists, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging. That odor, invisible and impinging, which impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge on the nose, which is invisible and impinging. This odor is the odor base, this is the odor element – this is that matter, the odor base.
^1-626

What is that matter which is the odor base?
That odor, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging: root odor, wood odor, bark odor, leaf odor, flower odor, fruit odor, raw odor, putrid odor, pleasant odor, unpleasant odor. Or whatever other odor exists, derived from the four great essential elements, invisible and impinging. That odor, having taken it as an object and relying on the nose, nose-contact arose, arises, will arise, or may arise; feeling born of nose-contact, perception, volition, nose-consciousness arose, arises, will arise, or may arise. That odor as its object, relying on the nose, nose-contact arose, arises, will arise, or may arise; feeling born of nose-contact, perception, volition, nose-consciousness arose, arises, will arise, or may arise. This odor is the odor base, this is the odor element – this is that matter, the odor base.
^1-627

What is the material phenomenon that is the taste-base?
That taste, derived from the four great primaries, invisible and impinging, such as root taste, trunk taste, bark taste, leaf taste, flower taste, fruit taste, sour, sweet, bitter, pungent, salty, alkaline, acrid, astringent, agreeable, disagreeable, or any other taste derived from the four great primaries, invisible and impinging, which taste, invisible and impinging, one has tasted, tastes, will taste, or may taste with the invisible and impinging tongue—this is taste, this is the taste-base, this is the taste-element. This is that material phenomenon which is the taste-base.
^1-628

What is the material phenomenon that is the taste-base?
That taste, derived from the four great primaries, invisible and impinging, such as root taste, trunk taste, bark taste, leaf taste, flower taste, fruit taste, sour, sweet, bitter, pungent, salty, alkaline, acrid, astringent, agreeable, disagreeable, or any other taste derived from the four great primaries, invisible and impinging, against which taste, invisible and impinging, the invisible and impinging tongue has impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge—this is taste, this is the taste-base, this is the taste-element. This is that material phenomenon which is the taste-base.
^1-629

What is the material phenomenon that is the taste-base?
That taste, derived from the four great primaries, invisible and impinging, such as root taste, trunk taste, bark taste, leaf taste, flower taste, fruit taste, sour, sweet, bitter, pungent, salty, alkaline, acrid, astringent, agreeable, disagreeable, or any other taste derived from the four great primaries, invisible and impinging, which taste, invisible and impinging, has impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge on the invisible and impinging tongue—this is taste, this is the taste-base, this is the taste-element. This is that material phenomenon which is the taste-base.
^1-630

What is the material phenomenon that is the taste-base?
That taste, derived from the four great primaries, invisible and impinging, such as root taste, trunk taste, bark taste, leaf taste, flower taste, fruit taste, sour, sweet, bitter, pungent, salty, alkaline, acrid, astringent, agreeable, disagreeable, or any other taste derived from the four great primaries, invisible and impinging, in consequence of which taste and depending on the tongue, gustatory contact has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
and, born of that gustatory contact, a feeling has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
[or] a perception
[or] volition
[or] gustatory consciousness;
further, having taste as its object and depending on the tongue, gustatory contact has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
and, born of that gustatory contact, a feeling has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
[or] a perception
[or] volition
[or] gustatory consciousness;
this is taste, this is the taste-base, this is the taste-element. This is that material phenomenon which is the taste-base.
^1-631

What is the material phenomenon that is the faculty of femininity?
That which is of the female, the female sign, female mark, female characteristic, female deportment, femininity, female state—this is that material phenomenon which is the faculty of femininity.
^1-632

What is the material phenomenon that is the faculty of masculinity?
That which is of the male, the male sign, male mark, male characteristic, male deportment, masculinity, male state—this is that material phenomenon which is the faculty of masculinity.
^1-633

What is the material phenomenon that is the life faculty?
That which is the duration, subsistence, maintenance, sustenance, activity, continuance, preservation, life, life faculty of these material phenomena—this is that material phenomenon which is the life faculty.
^1-634

What is the material phenomenon that is bodily intimation?
That tension, intentness, stiffening of the body, in one who is advancing or retreating, looking or glancing around, bending or stretching, due to a wholesome mind or unwholesome mind or indeterminate mind; the intimation, the making known, the state of having made known—this is that material phenomenon which is bodily intimation.
^1-635

What is the material phenomenon that is vocal intimation?
That speech, voice, enunciation, utterance, noise, making noises, language as articulate speech, which is of a wholesome mind or unwholesome mind or indeterminate mind—this is called speech. And that intimation, making known, state of having made known by that speech—this is that material phenomenon which is vocal intimation.
^1-636

What is the material phenomenon that is the space element?
That which is space, spatiality, non-obstruction, aperture, uncontacted by the four great primaries—this is that material phenomenon which is the space element.
^1-637

What is that matter which is the lightness of matter?
That lightness of matter which is quick changeability, non-sluggishness, non-stiffness—this is that matter which is the lightness of matter.
^1-638

What is that matter which is the pliancy of matter?
That pliancy of matter which is softness, non-harshness, non-rigidity—this is that matter which is the pliancy of matter.
^1-639

What is that matter which is the wieldiness of matter?
That wieldiness of matter which is fitness for work, suitability—this is that matter which is the wieldiness of matter.
^1-640

What is that matter which is the growth of matter?
That which is the accumulation of the sense bases, that is the growth of matter—this is that matter which is the growth of matter.
^1-641

What is that matter which is the continuity of matter?
That which is the growth of matter, that is the continuity of matter—this is that matter which is the continuity of matter.
^1-642

What is that matter which is the decay of matter?
That decay of matter which is old age, decay, brokenness, greyness, wrinkling of skin, decline of life, ripening of the faculties—this is that matter which is the decay of matter.
^1-643

What is that matter which is the impermanence of matter?
That impermanence of matter which is destruction, passing away, breaking up, complete breaking up, impermanence, disappearance—this is that matter which is the impermanence of matter.
^1-644

What is that matter which is edible food?
Rice, gruel, flour, fish, meat, milk, curds, ghee, fresh butter, oil, honey, molasses, or any other matter which in any country serves as food for beings, to be put into the mouth, chewed by the teeth, swallowed down the throat, filling the stomach, by the nutrient essence of which beings subsist—this is that matter which is edible food.
This is that matter which is derived.
The section on derived matter.
First section for recitation in the Chapter on Materiality.
^1-645

What is that materiality not clung to? The tangible base, the water element.
^1-646

What is that materiality, the tangible base? The earth element, fire element, air element; hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant to touch, painful to touch, heavy, light; that tangible, non-apparent, impinging, which the non-apparent, impinging body has touched, touches, will touch, or may touch—this is that materiality, the tangible base.
^1-647

What is that materiality, the tangible base? The earth element, fire element, air element; hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant to touch, painful to touch, heavy, light; that tangible, non-apparent, impinging, against which the non-apparent, impinging body has impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge—this is that materiality, the tangible base.
^1-648

What is that materiality, the tangible base? The earth element, fire element, air element; hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant to touch, painful to touch, heavy, light; that tangible, non-apparent, impinging, which has impinged, impinges, will impinge, or may impinge on the non-apparent, impinging body—this is that materiality, the tangible base.
^1-649

What is that materiality, the tangible base? The earth element, fire element, air element; hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant to touch, painful to touch, heavy, light; that tangible, concerning which, depending on the body, bodily contact has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
and, born of that bodily contact, feeling has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
perception has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
volition has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
body-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
further, that which has the tangible as its object, depending on the body, bodily contact has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
and, born of that bodily contact, feeling has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
perception has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
volition has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise
body-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise, or may arise—this is that materiality, the tangible base.
^1-650

What is that materiality, the water element? That which is water, watery, cohesion, cohesive, the binding quality of materiality—this is that materiality, the water element.
This is that materiality not clung to.
^1-651

What is that materiality clung to? The eye base, ear base, nose base, tongue base, body base, femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty; or whatever other materiality there is produced by kamma, visible object base, smell base, taste base, tangible base, space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, bodily nutriment—this is that materiality clung to.
^1-652

What is that materiality not clung to? The sound base, bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of materiality, malleability of materiality, wieldiness of materiality, decay of materiality, impermanence of materiality; or whatever other materiality there is not produced by kamma, visible object base, smell base, taste base, tangible base, space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, bodily nutriment—this is that materiality not clung to.
^1-653

What is that matter which is grasped and graspable?
The eye base...pe...body base, femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty; or whatever other matter exists due to kamma, including visible form base, smell base, taste base, tangible base, space element, water element, production of matter, continuity of matter, and edible food—this is that matter which is grasped and graspable.
^1-654

What is that matter which is ungrasped but graspable?
Sound base, bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter; or whatever other matter exists not due to kamma, including visible form base, smell base, taste base, tangible base, space element, water element, production of matter, continuity of matter, and edible food—this is that matter which is ungrasped but graspable.
^1-655

What is that matter which is visible?
The visible form base—this is that matter which is visible.
^1-656

What is that matter which is invisible?
The eye base...pe...edible food—this is that matter which is invisible.
^1-657

What is that matter with impingement?
The eye base, ear base, nose base, tongue base, body base, visible form base, sound base, smell base, taste base, tangible base—this is that matter with impingement.
^1-658

What is that matter without impingement?
The femininity faculty...pe...edible food—this is that matter without impingement.
^1-659

What is that matter which is a faculty?
The eye faculty, ear faculty, nose faculty, tongue faculty, body faculty, femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty—this is that matter which is a faculty.
^1-660

What is that matter which is not a faculty?
The visible form base...pe...edible food—this is that matter which is not a faculty.
^1-661

What is that matter which is a great element?
The tangible base and the water element—this is that matter which is a great element.
^1-662

What is that matter which is not a great element?
The eye base...pe...edible food—this is that matter which is not a great element.
^1-663

What is that material form which is intimation?
Bodily intimation, vocal intimation—this is that material form which is intimation.
^1-664

What is that material form which is not intimation?
The eye-base...edible food—this is that material form which is not intimation.
^1-665

What is that material form which is mind-originated?
Bodily intimation, vocal intimation, or whatever other material form exists that is born of mind, caused by mind, has its source in mind: visible form, sound, smell, taste, tangible object, space element, water element, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, production of matter, continuity of matter, edible food—this is that material form which is mind-originated.
^1-666

What is that material form which is not mind-originated?
The eye-base...body-base, femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty, decay of matter, impermanence of matter, or whatever other material form exists that is not born of mind, not caused by mind, does not have its source in mind: visible form, sound, smell, taste, tangible object, space element, water element, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, production of matter, continuity of matter, edible food—this is that material form which is not mind-originated.
^1-667

What is that material form which is co-arising with mind?
Bodily intimation, vocal intimation—this is that material form which is co-arising with mind.
^1-668

What is that material form which is not co-arising with mind?
The eye-base...edible food—this is that material form which is not co-arising with mind.
^1-669

What is that material form which is following the mind?
Bodily intimation, vocal intimation—this is that material form which is following the mind.
^1-670

What is that material form which is not following the mind?
The eye-base...edible food—this is that material form which is not following the mind.
^1-671

What is that material form which is internal?
The eye-base...body-base—this is that material form which is internal.
^1-672

What is that material form which is external?
Visible form...edible food—this is that material form which is external.
^1-673

What is that gross material form? The eye-base... and so on... the tangible-base—this is that gross material form.
^1-674

What is that subtle material form? The femininity faculty... and so on... nutriment—this is that subtle material form.
^1-675

What is that distant material form? The femininity faculty... and so on... nutriment—this is that distant material form.
^1-676

What is that near material form? The eye-base... and so on... the tangible-base—this is that near material form.
^1-677

What is that material form which is the basis of eye-contact? The eye-base—this is that material form which is the basis of eye-contact.
^1-678

What is that material form which is not the basis of eye-contact? The ear-base... and so on... nutriment—this is that material form which is not the basis of eye-contact.
^1-679

What is that material form which is the basis of the feeling... and so on... the perception... and so on... the volition... and so on... the eye-consciousness born of eye-contact? The eye-base—this is that material form which is the basis of eye-consciousness.
^1-680

What is that material form which is not the basis of eye-consciousness? The ear-base... and so on... nutriment—this is that material form which is not the basis of eye-consciousness.
^1-681

What is that material form which is the basis of ear-contact... and so on... nose-contact... and so on... tongue-contact... and so on... body-contact? The body-base—this is that material form which is the basis of body-contact.
^1-682

What is that material form which is not the basis of body-contact? The eye-base... and so on... nutriment—this is that material form which is not the basis of body-contact.
^1-683

What is that material form which is the basis of
the feeling…
the perception…
the volition…
the body-consciousness
that is born of bodily contact?
The body-base—this is that material form which is the basis for body-consciousness.
^1-684

What is that material form which is not the basis for body-consciousness?
The eye-base… and edible food—this is that material form which is not the basis for body-consciousness.
^1-685

What is that material form which is the object in visual contact?
The visible form-base—this is that material form which is the object in visual contact.
^1-686

What is that material form which is not the object in visual contact?
The eye-base… and edible food—this is that material form which is not the object in visual contact.
^1-687

What is that material form which is the object in
the feeling…
the perception…
the volition…
the eye-consciousness
that is born of visual contact?
The visible form-base—this is that material form which is the object for eye-consciousness.
^1-688

What is that material form which is not the object for eye-consciousness?
The eye-base… and edible food—this is that material form which is not the object for eye-consciousness.
^1-689

What is that material form which is the object in
auditory…
olfactory…
gustatory…
bodily
contact?
The tangible-base—this is that material form which is the object in bodily contact.
^1-690

What is that material form which is not the object in bodily contact?
The eye-base… and edible food—this is that material form which is not the object in bodily contact.
^1-691

What is that material form which is the object in
the feeling…
the perception…
the volition…
the body-consciousness
that is born of bodily contact?
The tangible-base—this is that material form which is the object for body-consciousness.
^1-692

What is that material form which is not the object for body-consciousness?
The eye-base… and edible food—this is that material form which is not the object for body-consciousness.
^1-693

What is that material form which is the eye sense base? That eye, which is sensitive matter derived from the four great primaries… like an empty village—this is that material form which is the eye sense base.
^1-694

What is that material form which is not the eye sense base? The ear sense base… and edible food—this is that material form which is not the eye sense base.
^1-695

What is that material form which is the ear sense base… the nose sense base… the tongue sense base… the body sense base? That body, which is sensitive matter derived from the four great primaries… like an empty village—this is that material form which is the body sense base.
^1-696

What is that material form which is not the body sense base? The eye sense base… and edible food—this is that material form which is not the body sense base.
^1-697

What is that material form which is the visible form sense base? That visible form, which is color and appearance derived from the four great primaries… visible form element—this is that material form which is the visible form sense base.
^1-698

What is that material form which is not the visible form sense base? The eye sense base… and edible food—this is that material form which is not the visible form sense base.
^1-699

What is that material form which is the sound sense base… the smell sense base… the taste sense base… the tangible sense base? The earth element… tangible element—this is that material form which is the tangible sense base.
^1-700

What is that material form which is not the tangible sense base? The eye sense base… and edible food—this is that material form which is not the tangible sense base.
^1-701

What is that material form which is the eye element? The eye sense base—this is that material form which is the eye element.
^1-702

What is that material form which is not the eye element? The ear sense base… and edible food—this is that material form which is not the eye element.
^1-703

What is that material form which is the ear-element 
 the nose-element 
 the tongue-element 
 the body-element? The body-base—this material form is the body-element.
^1-704

What is that material form which is not the body-element? The eye-base 
 and edible food—this material form is not the body-element.
^1-705

What is that material form which is the visible-form-element? The visible-form-base—this material form is the visible-form-element.
^1-706

What is that material form which is not the visible-form-element? The eye-base 
 and edible food—this material form is not the visible-form-element.
^1-707

 the smell-element 
 the taste-element 
 the tangible-element? The tangible-base—this material form is the tangible-element.
^1-708

What is that material form which is not the tangible-element? The eye-base 
 and edible food—this material form is not the tangible-element.
^1-709

What is that material form which is the eye-faculty? That eye, which is the sensitive matter derived from the four great primaries 
 like an empty village—this material form is the eye-faculty.
^1-710

What is that material form which is not the eye-faculty? The ear-base 
 and edible food—this material form is not the eye-faculty.
^1-711

 the nose-faculty 
 the tongue-faculty 
 the body-faculty? That body, which is the sensitive matter derived from the four great primaries 
 like an empty village—this material form is the body-faculty.
^1-712

What is that material form which is not the body-faculty? The eye-base 
 and edible food—this material form is not the body-faculty.
^1-713

What is that material phenomenon, the female faculty?
That which is a woman's female sign, female mark, female characteristic, female appearance, femaleness, female state—this is that material phenomenon, the female faculty.
^1-714

What is that material phenomenon which is not the female faculty?
The eye-base...pe...edible food—this is that material phenomenon which is not the female faculty.
^1-715

What is that material phenomenon, the male faculty?
That which is a man's male sign, male mark, male characteristic, male appearance, maleness, male state—this is that material phenomenon, the male faculty.
^1-716

What is that material phenomenon which is not the male faculty?
The eye-base...pe...edible food—this is that material phenomenon which is not the male faculty.
^1-717

What is that material phenomenon, the life faculty?
That which is the life, duration, maintenance, sustenance, carrying on, continuance, preservation, life, life faculty of those material phenomena—this is that material phenomenon, the life faculty.
^1-718

What is that material phenomenon which is not the life faculty?
The eye-base...pe...edible food—this is that material phenomenon which is not the life faculty.
^1-719

What is that material phenomenon, bodily intimation?
That which, when a wholesome mind, or an unwholesome mind, or an indeterminate mind is advancing or retreating, looking forward or around, bending or stretching, is the body's stiffening, making stiff, state of being stiffened, intimation, making known, state of being made known—this is that material phenomenon, bodily intimation.
^1-720

What is that material phenomenon which is not bodily intimation?
The eye-base...pe...edible food—this is that material phenomenon which is not bodily intimation.
^1-721

What is that material phenomenon, vocal intimation?
That which, when a wholesome mind, or an unwholesome mind, or an indeterminate mind, is speech, utterance, declaration, articulation, sound, act of sounding, speech, differentiation of speech—this is called speech. That which is the intimation, making known, state of being made known by that speech—this is that material phenomenon, vocal intimation.
^1-722

What is that material phenomenon which is not vocal intimation?
The eye-base...pe...edible food—this is that material phenomenon which is not vocal intimation.
^1-723

What is that matter, the space element?
That space, spatial, gap, characterized by gap, opening, characterized by opening, uncontacted by the four great elements – this is that matter, the space element.
^1-724

What is that matter which is not the space element?
The eye-base… up to edible food – this is that matter which is not the space element.
^1-725

What is that matter, the water element?
That water, watery, cohesion, characterized by cohesion, the binding nature of matter – this is that matter, the water element.
^1-726

What is that matter which is not the water element?
The eye-base… up to edible food – this is that matter which is not the water element.
^1-727

What is that matter, the lightness of matter?
That lightness of matter, quick changeability, non-sluggishness, non-expansiveness – this is that matter, the lightness of matter.
^1-728

What is that matter which is not the lightness of matter?
The eye-base… up to edible food – this is that matter which is not the lightness of matter.
^1-729

What is that matter, the malleability of matter?
That malleability of matter, softness, non-harshness, non-stiffness – this is that matter, the malleability of matter.
^1-730

What is that matter which is not the malleability of matter?
The eye-base… up to edible food – this is that matter which is not the malleability of matter.
^1-731

What is that matter, the workability of matter?
That workability of matter, suitability for action, state of being workable – this is that matter, the workability of matter.
^1-732

What is that matter which is not the workability of matter?
The eye-base… up to edible food – this is that matter which is not the workability of matter.
^1-733

What is the growth of matter? That which is the accumulation of the bases is the growth of matter. This is the growth of matter.
^1-734

What is not the growth of matter? The eye-base…etc.…edible food—this is not the growth of matter.
^1-735

What is the continuity of matter? That which is the growth of matter is the continuity of matter. This is the continuity of matter.
^1-736

What is not the continuity of matter? The eye-base…etc.…edible food—this is not the continuity of matter.
^1-737

What is the aging of matter? That which is the decay of matter, its wearing out, breaking up, greying, wrinkling, decline of life-span, and ripening of the faculties—this is the aging of matter.
^1-738

What is not the aging of matter? The eye-base…etc.…edible food—this is not the aging of matter.
^1-739

What is the impermanence of matter? That which is the wearing away of matter, its dissolution, breaking up, complete breaking up, impermanence, and disappearance—this is the impermanence of matter.
^1-740

What is not the impermanence of matter? The eye-base…etc.…edible food—this is not the impermanence of matter.
^1-741

What is edible food? Rice, gruel, flour, fish, meat, milk, curd, ghee, fresh butter, oil, honey, molasses, or whatever other matter exists in any country, which is put into the mouth, chewed by the teeth, swallowed down the throat, filling the stomach, by the nutrient essence of which beings subsist—this is edible food.
^1-742

What is not edible food? The eye-base…etc.…the impermanence of matter—this is not edible food.
Thus, the compendium of matter by dyads.
Explanation by Dyads.
^1-743

##### Explanation by Triads. ^1-2-3-4-0

What is that matter which is
internal and derived?
The eye base...pe...the body base. This is that matter which is internal and derived.

The eye base...pe...the body base. This is that matter which is internal and grasped.

What is that matter which is
external and grasped?
The femininity faculty, the masculinity faculty, the life faculty, or whatever other matter exists as karmically produced; the visible form base, the smell base, the taste base, the tangible object base, the space element, the water element, the production of matter, the continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter which is external and grasped.
^1-748

What is that matter which is
external and not grasped?
The sound base, bodily intimation, vocal intimation, the lightness of matter, the malleability of matter, the wieldiness of matter, the decay of matter, the impermanence of matter, or whatever other matter exists as not karmically produced; the visible form base, the smell base, the taste base, the tangible object base, the space element, the water element, the production of matter, the continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter which is external and not grasped.
^1-749

The eye base...pe...the body base. This is that matter which is internal, grasped, and graspable.

What is that matter which is external, clung-to, and graspable?
The femininity faculty, the masculinity faculty, the life faculty; and any other matter produced by kamma: the visible form sphere, the smell sphere, the taste sphere, the tangible sphere, the space element, the water element, the growth of matter, the continuity of matter, edible food—this is that matter which is external, clung-to, and graspable.
^1-751

The sound sphere, bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter; and any other matter not produced by kamma: the visible form sphere, the smell sphere, the taste sphere, the tangible sphere, the space element, the water element, the growth of matter, the continuity of matter, edible food—this is that matter which is external, not clung-to, but graspable.

What is that internal matter that is not the space element? Eye base...body base. This is that internal matter that is not the space element.
^1-852

What is that external matter which is the space element? That space, space-related, obstruction, obstruction-related, aperture, aperture-related, not touched by the four great elements. This is that external matter which is the space element.
^1-853

What is that external matter that is not the space element? Visible form base...edible food. This is that external matter that is not the space element.
^1-854

What is that internal matter that is not the water element? Eye base...body base. This is that internal matter that is not the water element.
^1-855

What is that external matter which is the water element? That water, water-related, cohesion, cohesion-related, the binding nature of matter. This is that external matter which is the water element.
^1-856

What is that external matter that is not the water element? Visible form base...edible food. This is that external matter that is not the water element.
^1-857

What is that internal matter that is not the lightness of matter? Eye base...body base. This is that internal matter that is not the lightness of matter.
^1-858

What is that external matter which is the lightness of matter? That lightness of matter, quick changeability, non-sluggishness, non-unwieldiness. This is that external matter which is the lightness of matter.
^1-859

What is that external matter that is not the lightness of matter? Visible form base...edible food. This is that external matter that is not the lightness of matter.
^1-860

What is that internal matter which is not pliability of matter? The eye-base... up to... the body-base 
– this is that internal matter which is not pliability of matter.
^1-861

What is that external matter which is pliability of matter? That pliability of matter which is softness, non-harshness, non-stiffness 
– this is that external matter which is pliability of matter.
^1-862

What is that external matter which is not pliability of matter? The visible-form-base... up to... nutritive essence 
– this is that external matter which is not pliability of matter.
^1-863

What is that internal matter which is not workability of matter? The eye-base... up to... the body-base 
– this is that internal matter which is not workability of matter.
^1-864

What is that external matter which is workability of matter? That workability of matter which is adaptability, fitness for action 
– this is that external matter which is workability of matter.
^1-865

What is that external matter which is not workability of matter? The visible-form-base... up to... nutritive essence 
– this is that external matter which is not workability of matter.
^1-866

What is that internal matter which is not growth of matter? The eye-base... up to... the body-base 
– this is that internal matter which is not growth of matter.
^1-867

What is that external matter which is growth of matter? That which is the accumulation of bases, that is the growth of matter 
– this is that external matter which is growth of matter.
^1-868

What is that external matter which is not growth of matter? The visible-form-base... up to... nutritive essence 
– this is that external matter which is not growth of matter.
^1-869

What is that internal matter which is not continuity of matter? The eye-base... up to... the body-base 
– this is that internal matter which is not continuity of matter.
^1-870

What is that external matter which is the continuity of matter? That which is the production of matter, that is the continuity of matter – this is that external matter which is the continuity of matter.
^1-871

What is that external matter which is not the continuity of matter? Visible form...
edible food – this is that external matter which is not the continuity of matter.
^1-872

What is that internal matter which is not the aging of matter? Eye-base...
body-base – this is that internal matter which is not the aging of matter.
^1-873

What is that external matter which is the aging of matter? That which is the decay of matter, its wearing out, brokenness, graying, wrinkling of skin, decline of life, ripening of faculties – this is that external matter which is the aging of matter.
^1-874

What is that external matter which is not the aging of matter? Visible form...
edible food – this is that external matter which is not the aging of matter.
^1-875

What is that internal matter which is not the impermanence of matter? Eye-base...
body-base – this is that internal matter which is not the impermanence of matter.
^1-876

What is that external matter which is the impermanence of matter? That which is the wasting away of matter, its dissolution, breaking up, complete breaking up, impermanence, disappearance – this is that external matter which is the impermanence of matter.
^1-877

What is that external matter which is not the impermanence of matter? Visible form...
edible food – this is that external matter which is not the impermanence of matter.
^1-878

What is that internal matter which is not edible food? Eye-base...
body-base – this is that internal matter which is not edible food.
^1-879

What is that external matter which is edible food? Cooked rice, gruel, flour, fish, meat, milk, curd, ghee, fresh butter, oil, honey, treacle, or whatever other matter exists which, in various countries, is taken into the mouth by various beings, chewed by the teeth, swallowed down the throat, filling the stomach, by the nutrient essence of which beings subsist – this is that external matter which is edible food.
^1-880

Which matter is external and not physical nutriment? Visible form... up to... the impermanence of matter – this is that matter which is external and not physical nutriment.
Thus, the compendium of matter is in three ways.
Explanation of the Triads.
^1-881

##### Tetrads. ^1-2-3-5-0

Which matter is sustained by clinging and grasped by kamma? Eye-base... up to... body-base, femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty, or whatever other matter there is that is kamma-produced: visible form base, smell base, taste base, space element, production of matter, continuity of matter, physical nutriment – this is that matter sustained by clinging and grasped by kamma.
^1-882

Which matter is sustained by clinging but not grasped by kamma? Sound base, bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter, or whatever other matter there is that is not kamma-produced: visible form base, smell base, taste base, space element, production of matter, continuity of matter, physical nutriment – this is that matter sustained by clinging but not grasped by kamma.
^1-883

Which matter is not sustained by clinging but grasped by kamma? The tangible base and water element that are kamma-produced – this is that matter not sustained by clinging but grasped by kamma.
^1-884

Which matter is neither sustained by clinging nor grasped by kamma? The tangible base and water element that are not kamma-produced – this is that matter neither sustained by clinging nor grasped by kamma.
^1-885

Which matter is sustained by clinging, grasped by kamma, and an object of clinging? Eye-base... up to... body-base, femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty, or whatever other matter there is that is kamma-produced: visible form base, smell base, taste base, space element, production of matter, continuity of matter, physical nutriment – this is that matter sustained by clinging, grasped by kamma, and an object of clinging.
^1-886

Which matter is sustained by clinging, not grasped by kamma, and an object of clinging? Sound base, bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter, or whatever other matter there is that is not kamma-produced: visible form base, smell base, taste base, space element, production of matter, continuity of matter, physical nutriment – this is that matter sustained by clinging, not grasped by kamma, and an object of clinging.
^1-887

 not derived, 
 grasped and graspable? The tangible sphere and the water element, due to being produced by kamma – this is that matter which is not derived, grasped and graspable.
^1-888

 not derived, 
 not grasped and graspable? The tangible sphere and the water element, not due to being produced by kamma – this is that matter which is not derived, not grasped and graspable.

 subtle? The water element – this is that matter which is not derived, subtle.

Water element. This is that material form not derived and far.

Tangible object. This is that material form not derived and near.

Visible form due to kamma. This is that material form grasped and visible.

Eye-base...pe... body-base, femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty, or whatever other material form exists due to kamma: smell-base, taste-base, tangible object, space element, water element, growth of matter, continuity of matter, nutritive essence. This is that material form grasped and invisible.

What is that material form not grasped and visible? 
Visible form not due to kamma. This is that material form not grasped and visible.

Sound-base, bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, pliancy of matter, wieldiness of matter, aging of matter, impermanence of matter, or whatever other material form exists not due to kamma: smell-base, taste-base, tangible object, space element, water element, growth of matter, continuity of matter, nutritive essence. This is that material form not grasped and invisible.
^1-905

Eye-base...pe... body-base, or whatever other material form exists due to kamma: visible form, smell-base, taste-base, tangible object. This is that material form grasped and with impingement.

What is that material form grasped and without impingement? 
Femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty, or whatever other material form exists due to kamma: space element, water element, growth of matter, continuity of matter, nutritive essence. This is that material form grasped and without impingement.

 Bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter, or any other matter not produced by kamma, such as the space element, water element, production of matter, continuity of matter, and edible food. This is that matter which is not grasped and without impingement.

 The tangible base and water element produced by kamma. This is that matter which is grasped and a great element.

 The eye base, ... the body base, the femininity faculty, the masculinity faculty, the life faculty, or any other matter produced by kamma, such as the visible form base, smell base, taste base, space element, production of matter, continuity of matter, and edible food. This is that matter which is grasped and not a great element.

 The femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty, or any other matter produced by kamma, such as the space element, water element, production of matter, continuity of matter, and edible food. This is that matter which is grasped and subtle.

 Bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter, or any other matter not produced by kamma, such as the space element, water element, production of matter, continuity of matter, and edible food. This is that matter which is not grasped and subtle.

^1-918

What is that matter that is grasped and near? The eye-base, ear-base, nose-base, tongue-base, body-base, or any other matter produced by kamma: visible form, smell, taste, tangible object. This is that matter that is grasped and near.
^1-919

What is that matter that is ungrasped and distant? Bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter, or any other matter not produced by kamma: the space element, the water element, the production of matter, the continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter that is ungrasped and distant.

The eye-base, ear-base, nose-base, tongue-base, body-base, the female faculty, the male faculty, the life faculty, or any other matter produced by kamma: smell, taste, tangible object, the space element, the water element, the production of matter, the continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter that is grasped, graspable, and invisible.

The sound-base, bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter, or any other matter not produced by kamma: smell, taste, tangible object, the space element, the water element, the production of matter, the continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter that is ungrasped, graspable, and invisible.

What is that matter that is grasped, graspable, and with impact? The eye-base, ear-base, nose-base, tongue-base, body-base, or any other matter produced by kamma: visible form, smell, taste, tangible object. This is that matter that is grasped, graspable, and with impact.
^1-926

What is that matter that is grasped, graspable, and without impact? The female faculty, the male faculty, the life faculty, or any other matter produced by kamma: the space element, the water element, the production of matter, the continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter which is not grasped/graspable and has no impact.

This is that matter which is grasped/graspable and is not a primary element.

Sound base, bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter, or any other matter not produced by kamma: visible form base, smell base, taste base, space element, origination of matter, continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter which is not grasped/graspable and is not a primary element.

What is that matter which is grasped/graspable and is gross? Eye base... body base, or any other matter produced by kamma: visible form base, smell base, taste base, tangible object base. This is that matter which is grasped/graspable and is gross.

What is that matter which is grasped/graspable and is subtle? Femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty, or any other matter produced by kamma: space element, water element, origination of matter, continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter which is grasped/graspable and is subtle.
^1-935

What is that matter which is not grasped/graspable and is gross? Sound base, or any other matter not produced by kamma: visible form base, smell base, taste base, tangible object base. This is that matter which is not grasped/graspable and is gross.
^1-936

What is that matter which is not grasped/graspable and is subtle? Bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter, or any other matter not produced by kamma: space element, water element, origination of matter, continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter which is not grasped/graspable and is subtle.

What is that matter, grasped and graspable, distant? The femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty, or any other matter that exists due to kamma: the space element, water element, production of matter, continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter, grasped and graspable, distant.
^1-938

What is that matter, grasped and graspable, near? The eye base... to... the body base, or any other matter that exists due to kamma: the visible form base, smell base, taste base, tangible object base. This is that matter, grasped and graspable, near.
^1-939

What is that matter, ungrasped and ungraspable, distant? Bodily intimation, vocal intimation, lightness of matter, malleability of matter, wieldiness of matter, decay of matter, impermanence of matter, or any other matter that exists not due to kamma: the space element, water element, production of matter, continuity of matter, edible food. This is that matter, ungrasped and ungraspable, distant.
^1-940

What is that matter, ungrasped and ungraspable, near? The sound base, or any other matter that exists not due to kamma: the visible form base, smell base, taste base, tangible object base. This is that matter, ungrasped and ungraspable, near.
^1-941

What is that matter, impinging and a faculty? The eye faculty... to... the body faculty. This is that matter, impinging and a faculty.
^1-942

What is that matter, impinging and not a faculty? The visible form base... to... the tangible object base. This is that matter, impinging and not a faculty.
^1-943

What is that matter, non-impinging and a faculty? The femininity faculty, masculinity faculty, life faculty. This is that matter, non-impinging and a faculty.
^1-944

What is that matter, non-impinging and not a faculty? Bodily intimation, vocal intimation... to... edible food. This is that matter, non-impinging and not a faculty.

^1-946

What is that matter, impinging and not a great essential matter? The eye base... to... the taste base. This is that matter, impinging and not a great essential matter.
^1-947

The water element. This is that material phenomenon which is non-impinging and a primary element.

What is that material phenomenon which is non-impinging and not a primary element?
The femininity faculty…pe… nutriment taken in morsels. This is that material phenomenon which is non-impinging and not a primary element.

The eye faculty…pe… the body faculty. This is that material phenomenon which is a faculty and gross.

The femininity faculty, the masculinity faculty, the life faculty. This is that material phenomenon which is a faculty and subtle.

The visible object…pe… the tangible object. This is that material phenomenon which is not a faculty and gross.

Bodily intimation, verbal intimation…pe… nutriment taken in morsels. This is that material phenomenon which is not a faculty and subtle.

The femininity faculty, the masculinity faculty, the life faculty. This is that material phenomenon which is a faculty and remote.

The visible object…pe… the tangible object. This is that material phenomenon which is not a faculty and proximate.

^1-958

What is that matter which is a great element and is subtle? The water element. This is that matter which is a great element and is subtle.
^1-959

What is that matter which is not a great element but is gross? The eye sense base... the taste sense base. This is that matter which is not a great element but is gross.
^1-960

What is that matter which is not a great element and is subtle? The female faculty... nutritive essence. This is that matter which is not a great element and is subtle.

^1-962

What is that matter which is a great element and is proximate? The tangible sense base. This is that matter which is a great element and is proximate.
^1-963

What is that matter which is not a great element and is remote? The female faculty... nutritive essence. This is that matter which is not a great element and is remote.

^1-965

Visible form is seen,
sound is heard,
smell, taste, and tangible objects are sensed,
all matter is matter cognized by the mind.
Thus, the compilation of matter is fourfold.
The Tetrad.
^1-966

##### The Pentad ^1-2-3-6-0

What is the matter that is the earth element? That which is hardness, hard, the state of hardness, the nature of hardness, whether internal or external, grasped or not grasped – this is that matter, the earth element.
^1-967

What is the matter that is the water element? That which is water, watery, cohesion, cohesive, the binding of matter, whether internal or external, grasped or not grasped – this is that matter, the water element.
^1-968

What is the matter that is the fire element? That which is fire, fiery, heat, hot, warmth, warm, whether internal or external, grasped or not grasped – this is that matter, the fire element.
^1-969

What is the matter that is the air element? That which is air, airy, the quality of support of matter, whether internal or external, grasped or not grasped – this is that matter, the air element.
^1-970

What is the derived matter? The eye-base… (and so on)… edible food – this is that derived matter.
Thus, the compilation of matter is fivefold.
The Pentad.
^1-971

##### The Hexad ^1-2-3-7-0

Visual form is form cognizable by eye-consciousness; sound form is form cognizable by ear-consciousness; odour form is form cognizable by nose-consciousness; taste form is form cognizable by tongue-consciousness; tangible form is form cognizable by body-consciousness; all form is form cognizable by mind-consciousness.
Thus is the compendium of matter in six ways.
The Sixfold Way
^1-972

##### The Sevenfold Way ^1-2-3-8-0

Visual form is form cognizable by eye-consciousness; sound form is form cognizable by ear-consciousness; odour form is form cognizable by nose-consciousness; taste form is form cognizable by tongue-consciousness; tangible form is form cognizable by body-consciousness; visual, sound, odour, taste, and tangible forms are forms cognizable by the mind-element consciousness; all form is form cognizable by the mind-consciousness-element consciousness.
Thus is the compendium of matter in seven ways.
The Sevenfold Way
^1-973

##### The Eightfold Way ^1-2-3-9-0

Visual form is form cognizable by eye-consciousness; sound form is form cognizable by ear-consciousness; odour form is form cognizable by nose-consciousness; taste form is form cognizable by tongue-consciousness; agreeable tangible form, pleasant contact, is form cognizable by body-consciousness; disagreeable tangible form, painful contact, is form cognizable by body-consciousness; visual, sound, odour, taste, and tangible forms are forms cognizable by the mind-element consciousness; all form is form cognizable by the mind-consciousness-element consciousness.
Thus is the compendium of matter in eight ways.
The Eightfold Group.
^1-974

##### The Ninefold Group ^1-2-3-10-0

What is that material form which is the eye-faculty? That eye, which is sensitive matter derived from the four great elements…pe… this is that material form which is the eye-faculty.
^1-975

What is that material form which is the ear-faculty…pe… the nose-faculty…pe… the tongue-faculty…pe… the body-faculty…pe… the female-faculty…pe… the male-faculty…pe… the life-faculty? That which is the life, standing, maintaining, sustaining, carrying on, continuing, protecting, the life of those material phenomena, the life-faculty – this is that material form which is the life-faculty.
^1-976

What is that material form which is not a faculty? Visual form…pe… edible food – this is that material form which is not a faculty.
Thus, the collection of material forms is in nine ways.
The Ninefold Group.
^1-977

##### The Tenfold Group ^1-2-3-11-0

What is that material form which is the eye-faculty? That eye, which is sensitive matter derived from the four great elements…pe… this is that material form which is the eye-faculty.
^1-978

What is that material form which is the ear-faculty…pe… the nose-faculty…pe… the tongue-faculty…pe… the body-faculty…pe… the female-faculty…pe… the male-faculty…pe… the life-faculty? That which is the life, standing, maintaining, sustaining, carrying on, continuing, protecting, the life of those material phenomena, the life-faculty – this is that material form which is the life-faculty.
^1-979

What is that material phenomenon which is not a faculty and has impingement? Visible object... up to... tangible object – this is that material phenomenon which is not a faculty and has impingement.
^1-980

What is that material phenomenon which is not a faculty and does not have impingement? Bodily intimation... up to... nutritive essence – this is that material phenomenon which is not a faculty and does not have impingement.
Thus, the collection of material phenomena is tenfold.
The Decad.
^1-981

##### The Undecad ^1-2-3-12-0

What is that material phenomenon which is the eye-base? That eye which is a sensitive part dependent on the four great primaries... up to... a deserted village – this is that material phenomenon which is the eye-base.
^1-982

What is that material phenomenon which is the ear-base... up to... the tangible object-base? Earth element... up to... tangible element – this is that material phenomenon which is the tangible object-base.
^1-983

What is that material phenomenon which is non-manifesting, without impingement, and included in the dhamma-base? Femininity faculty... up to... nutritive essence – this is that material phenomenon which is non-manifesting, without impingement, and included in the dhamma-base.
Thus, the collection of material phenomena is elevenfold.
The Undecad.
Eighth Recitation Section.
Classification of Matter.
The Section on Matter is concluded.
^1-984

### 3. Compendium of Categories ^1-3-0

#### Exposition of Triads ^1-3-1-0

Which phenomena are wholesome? The three wholesome roots—non-greed, non-hatred, non-delusion; the feeling aggregate, perception aggregate, formation aggregate, and consciousness aggregate associated with them; and the bodily kamma, verbal kamma, and mental kamma arisen from them—these phenomena are wholesome.
^1-985

Which phenomena are unwholesome? The three unwholesome roots—greed, hatred, delusion; and the defilements co-existent with them; the feeling aggregate, perception aggregate, formation aggregate, and consciousness aggregate associated with them; and the bodily kamma, verbal kamma, and mental kamma arisen from them—these phenomena are unwholesome.
^1-986

Which phenomena are indeterminate? The resultants of wholesome and unwholesome phenomena in the sense sphere, fine-material sphere, immaterial sphere, and supramundane; the feeling aggregate, perception aggregate, formation aggregate, and consciousness aggregate; and those functional phenomena that are neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor kamma-resultants; and all matter, and the unconditioned element—these phenomena are indeterminate.
^1-987

Which phenomena are associated with pleasant feeling? In the plane of pleasant feeling, in the sense sphere, fine-material sphere, and supramundane, excluding pleasant feeling; the perception aggregate, formation aggregate, and consciousness aggregate associated with it—these phenomena are associated with pleasant feeling.
^1-988

Which phenomena are associated with painful feeling? In the plane of painful feeling, in the sense sphere, excluding painful feeling; the perception aggregate, formation aggregate, and consciousness aggregate associated with it—these phenomena are associated with painful feeling.
^1-989

Which phenomena are associated with neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling?
The aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, and the aggregate of consciousness, in the sense, fine-material, immaterial, and transcendent spheres, associated with neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling, excluding the feeling itself.
^1-990

Which phenomena are results?
The results of wholesome and unwholesome phenomena in the sense, fine-material, immaterial, and transcendent spheres, namely, the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, and the aggregate of consciousness.
^1-991

Which phenomena have the nature of producing results?
Wholesome and unwholesome phenomena in the sense, fine-material, immaterial, and transcendent spheres, namely, the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, and the aggregate of consciousness.
^1-992

Which phenomena are neither results nor having the nature of producing results?
Functional phenomena that are neither wholesome nor unwholesome and not kamma-results; all material phenomena; and the unconditioned element.
^1-993

Which phenomena are clung to and graspable?
The results of wholesome and unwholesome phenomena, subject to taints, in the sense, fine-material, and immaterial spheres, namely, the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, and the aggregate of consciousness; and matter born of kamma.
^1-994

Which phenomena are not clung to, but graspable?
Wholesome and unwholesome phenomena, subject to taints, in the sense, fine-material, and immaterial spheres, namely, the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, and the aggregate of consciousness; functional phenomena that are neither wholesome nor unwholesome and not kamma-results; and matter not born of kamma.
^1-995

Which phenomena are neither clung to nor graspable?
The transcendent paths, their fruits, and the unconditioned element.
^1-996

Which phenomena are defiled and defiling?
The three unwholesome roots – greed, hatred, delusion; associated defilements; the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, and the aggregate of consciousness associated with them; and bodily, verbal, and mental action arising from them.
^1-997

Which phenomena are undefiled but defiling?
Wholesome and indeterminate phenomena, subject to taints, in the sense, fine-material, and immaterial spheres, namely, the aggregate of matter, the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, and the aggregate of consciousness.
^1-998

Which phenomena are neither defiled nor defiling?
The transcendent paths, their fruits, and the unconditioned element.
^1-999

Which phenomena are accompanied by initial application and sustained application?
The feeling aggregate, perception aggregate, volitional formations aggregate, and consciousness aggregate, associated with them, in the sphere of initial and sustained application (sense sphere, fine-material sphere, and supramundane), excluding initial application and sustained application. These phenomena are accompanied by initial application and sustained application.
^1-1000

Which phenomena are without initial application, only sustained application?
The feeling aggregate, perception aggregate, volitional formations aggregate, and consciousness aggregate, associated with them, in the sphere of only sustained application (fine-material sphere and supramundane), excluding sustained application. These phenomena are without initial application, only sustained application.
^1-1001

Which phenomena are without initial application and without sustained application?
The feeling aggregate, perception aggregate, volitional formations aggregate, and consciousness aggregate, in the sphere without initial and sustained application (sense sphere, fine-material sphere, immaterial sphere, and supramundane); all material phenomena; and the unconditioned element. These phenomena are without initial application and without sustained application.
^1-1002

Which phenomena are accompanied by joy?
The feeling aggregate, perception aggregate, volitional formations aggregate, and consciousness aggregate, associated with them, in the sphere of joy (sense sphere, fine-material sphere, and supramundane), excluding joy. These phenomena are accompanied by joy.
^1-1003

Which phenomena are accompanied by happiness?
The perception aggregate, volitional formations aggregate, and consciousness aggregate, associated with them, in the sphere of happiness (sense sphere, fine-material sphere, and supramundane), excluding happiness. These phenomena are accompanied by happiness.
^1-1004

Which phenomena are accompanied by equanimity?
The perception aggregate, volitional formations aggregate, and consciousness aggregate, associated with them, in the sphere of equanimity (sense sphere, fine-material sphere, immaterial sphere, and supramundane), excluding equanimity. These phenomena are accompanied by equanimity.
^1-1005

Which phenomena are to be abandoned by vision?
The three fetters: personality view, doubt, and adherence to rites and rituals.
^1-1006

What is personality view?
Here, an uninstructed worldling, not seeing noble ones, unskilled in the Dhamma of noble ones, untrained in the Dhamma of noble ones; not seeing good men, unskilled in the Dhamma of good men, untrained in the Dhamma of good men, regards form as self, or self as having form, or form as in self, or self as in form; regards feeling as self, or self as having feeling, or feeling as in self, or self as in feeling; regards perception as self, or self as having perception, or perception as in self, or self as in perception; regards volitional formations as self, or self as having volitional formations, or volitional formations as in self, or self as in volitional formations; regards consciousness as self, or self as having consciousness, or consciousness as in self, or self as in consciousness. Whatever such view, wrong view, thicket of views, wilderness of views, distortion of views, vacillation of views, fetter of views, grasping, clinging, adherence, obsession, wrong path, false way, wrongness, sectarian position, inverted grasp – this is called personality view.
^1-1007

What is doubt?
Doubts, is perplexed about, the Teacher; doubts, is perplexed about, the Dhamma; doubts, is perplexed about, the Saṅgha; doubts, is perplexed about, the training; doubts, is perplexed about, the past; doubts, is perplexed about, the future; doubts, is perplexed about, both past and future; doubts, is perplexed about, dependent arising and dependently arisen phenomena. Whatever such wavering, state of wavering, perplexity, doubt, two-way path, uncertainty, grasping at many sides, vacillation, wandering, lack of penetration, stiffness of mind, mental defilement – this is called doubt.
^1-1008

What is adherence to rites and rituals?
Among recluses and brahmins outside this doctrine, the view 'purification by virtue, purification by rites, purification by virtue and rites'. Whatever such view, wrong view, thicket of views, wilderness of views, distortion of views, vacillation of views, fetter of views, grasping, clinging, adherence, obsession, wrong path, false way, wrongness, sectarian position, inverted grasp – this is called adherence to rites and rituals.
^1-1009

These three fetters; and defilements associated with them; the feeling aggregate... up to the consciousness aggregate associated with them; bodily action, vocal action, mental action arising from them – these phenomena are to be abandoned by seeing.
^1-1010

Which phenomena are to be abandoned by development? The remaining greed, hatred, delusion; and defilements associated with them; the feeling aggregate... up to the consciousness aggregate associated with them; bodily action, vocal action, mental action arising from them – these phenomena are to be abandoned by development.
^1-1011

Which phenomena are to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development? Wholesome and indeterminate phenomena belonging to the sense sphere, fine-material sphere, immaterial sphere, and unincluded; the feeling aggregate... up to the consciousness aggregate; and all matter, and the unconditioned element – these phenomena are to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development.
^1-1012

Which phenomena have causes to be abandoned by seeing? The three fetters: identity view, doubt, clinging to rites and rituals.
^1-1013

Therein, what is identity view? ... This is called identity view.
^1-1014

Therein, what is doubt? ... This is called doubt.
^1-1015

Therein, what is clinging to rites and rituals? ... This is called clinging to rites and rituals.
^1-1016

These three fetters; and defilements associated with them; the feeling aggregate... up to the consciousness aggregate associated with them; bodily action, vocal action, mental action arising from them – these phenomena have causes to be abandoned by seeing. The three fetters – identity view, doubt, clinging to rites and rituals – these phenomena are to be abandoned by seeing. Greed, hatred, delusion associated with them – these phenomena are the causes to be abandoned by seeing. And defilements associated with them; the feeling aggregate... up to the consciousness aggregate associated with them; bodily action, vocal action, mental action arising from them – these phenomena have causes to be abandoned by seeing.
^1-1017

Which phenomena have causes to be abandoned by development? The remaining greed, hatred, delusion – these phenomena are the causes to be abandoned by development. And defilements associated with them; the feeling aggregate... up to the consciousness aggregate associated with them; bodily action, vocal action, mental action arising from them – these phenomena have causes to be abandoned by development.
^1-1018

Which phenomena have causes to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development? Excluding those phenomena, the remaining wholesome, unwholesome, and indeterminate phenomena belonging to the sense sphere, fine-material sphere, immaterial sphere, and unincluded; the feeling aggregate, perception aggregate, formation aggregate, consciousness aggregate; and all matter, and the unconditioned element – these phenomena have causes to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development.
^1-1019

States that lead to accumulation are: Tainted wholesome and unwholesome phenomena of the sense sphere, fine-material sphere, and immaterial sphere; the feeling aggregate, perception aggregate, mental formations aggregate, and consciousness aggregate.
^1-1020

States that lead to reduction are: The four supramundane paths.
^1-1021
